in Lemma
2.3
and condition (

) on the multiplier

,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{itc_{l}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)e^{-itc_{m}|\xi-\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)e^{-itc_{n}|\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}}

Moreover, Lemma
2.4
gives us,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

Combing the results above and employ the bounds (
3.13
), (
3.2
), we get for any
t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}\|\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}

(3.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{\epsilon k_{-}}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M+k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, (
3.21
), (
3.26
), and (
3.27
) shows

\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M+k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

If we use the
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate in Lemma
2.3
,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(1+2^{\epsilon k_{-}})\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}.

By (
3.10
), (
3.2
), for any
t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}\|\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}\min\{\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-10k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{2}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-10k_{+}}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{2}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-10k_{+}-M-\gamma M/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next, we consider
\|e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{6}}=\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{6}}
. Using the
L^{6}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{6}

2.8
, Lemma
2.13
and (

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{6}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\epsilon k_{-}})\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-M}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6/5}}2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-5M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{1}/2},2^{-M+2k_{2}-k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Moreover, by Bernstein’s inequality, Lemma
2.4
and (
3.2
),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{6}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{6}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-it|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-it|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\epsilon k_{-}})2^{k+3k/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+5k/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus,

\displaystyle\|e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf1}\|_{L^{6}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\eqref{partialtf2}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-5M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{1}/2},2^{-M+2k_{2}-k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-5M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{1}/2},2^{5k/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2M-k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

∎

Based on the setup we used in Lemma
3.3
for
\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}
, we can further take one derivative in
\xi
and find bounds on the mixed derivatives of
\hat{f}_{l,k}
.

Lemma 3.4
.

Under the assumption (
1.8
), we have for any
t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-M-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

(3.28)

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-M-2k_{+}-k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(3.29)

Proof.

We split
\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{k}=F_{1,k}+F_{2,k}
to discuss the problem differently when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
and when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
for
F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
. Recall the equation of
\partial_{t}f_{k}
in (
3.17
) and define

\displaystyle F_{1,k}=\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}F^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}},\qquad F_{2,k}=\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}.

(3.30)

Hence, (
3.21
), (
3.26
), and (
3.27
) imply

\|F_{1,k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M+k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

(3.31)

and

\displaystyle\|F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\bigg\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it(c_{l}|\xi|^{2}-c_{m}|\xi-\eta|^{2}-c_{n}|\eta|^{2})}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(3.32)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k/2},2^{5k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{l}}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.33)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.34)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi),

(3.35)

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{l}}F^{2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.36)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.37)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.38)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi),

(3.39)

When
c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0
and
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
, we have (
3.22
) and may use the identity

\sum_{m=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}

to integrate by parts,

\displaystyle\eqref{mixed1.1}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.40)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.41)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi).

(3.42)

For the term (
3.36
),
\phi(\xi,\eta)=(c_{l}+c_{n})|\xi|^{2}-2c_{n}\xi\cdot\eta
since
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
. In this case, we may perform integration by parts using the identity
\sum_{m=1}^{3}-\frac{\xi_{m}}{it2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}

\displaystyle\eqref{mixed2.1}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.43)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.44)

,

By Lemma

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M-(1/2-\alpha)M-k/2+\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M+k-2\alpha k-k/2+\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle=2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

When

,
