hence

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+5k/2(1-\alpha)+\alpha 3k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+5k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Therefore,

\|D^{\alpha}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\leq 2^{-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k-M+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.52)

and (
3.48
) combined with (
3.52
) proves (
3.28
).

Moreover, from the computation above, we also have

\|\nabla_{\xi}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}-M-k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

(3.53)

and (
3.29
) is an immediate result of (
3.47
) and (
3.53
).
∎

Lastly, using parts of the estimates we obtained in Lemma
3.4
, we may conclude this section with the following result for the
L^{3}
norm of the mixed derivatives.

Lemma 3.5
.

Under the assumption (
1.8
), we have for any
t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]

\displaystyle\|e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t,\xi)\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim 2^{-M-2k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

We will keep the definition of
F_{1,k}
and
F_{2,k}
same as in the proof of Lemma
3.4
, then it suffices to show

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{1,k}\|_{L^{3}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim 2^{-M-2k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Using Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
2.11
and (
3.47
),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{1,k}\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim 2^{k/2}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{1,k}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-M-2k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next, from (
3.30
) we observe that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{3}}\leq\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}}

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-ic_{m}t|\xi-\eta|^{2}-ic_{n}t|\eta|^{2}}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.54)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{F}^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-ic_{m}t|\xi-\eta|^{2}-ic_{n}t|\eta|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)

(3.55)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{F}^{-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{-ic_{m}t|\xi-\eta|^{2}-ic_{n}t|\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi).

(3.56)

By Bernstein’s inequality,
(
3.49
)
, and
(
3.51
)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{l3mixed1.3}\|_{L^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{k/2}\bigg\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=2^{k/2}\|\eqref{mixed1.3}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{l3mixed1.2}\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k/2}\bigg\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)d\eta\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{k/2}\|\eqref{mixed1.2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{k/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-5k_{1}/6-2k_{2}/3},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Lastly, using the
L^{6}\times L^{6}\rightarrow L^{3}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.7
and Lemma
2.13
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{l3mixed1.1}\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+k-2M}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6/5}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6/5}}\lesssim 2^{-M+k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Summarizing the bounds above, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}e^{-ic_{l}t|\xi|^{2}}\nabla_{\xi}F_{2,k}\|_{L^{3}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}}\|\eqref{l3mixed1.1}+\eqref{l3mixed1.2}+\eqref{l3mixed1.3}\|_{L^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-M-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k},2^{2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-M-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-M-2k_{+}}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

∎

4
Proof of Theorem
1.1

Assume the initial condition
\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{0}
. The local well-posedness in Proposition
5.1
gives the bootstrap assumption (
1.8
).

First, we perform an energy estimate to bound the term
\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}=\|e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}f_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}=\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}
. Take any
t\in[1,T]
, by the initial condition (
1.6
), the assumption (
1.8
), and the estimation in Lemma
3.3

\displaystyle\|u_{l}(t,x)\|^{2}_{H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{l0}\|^{2}_{H^{10}_{x}}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}\partial_{s}\|f_{l,k}(s,x)\|^{2}_{H^{10}_{x}}ds

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}\partial_{s}\|(1+|\xi|^{2})^{5}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\xi}}ds

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}\partial_{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|\xi|^{2})^{10}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\bar{\hat{f}}_{l,k}(s,\xi)d\xi ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}2^{20k_{+}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{s}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\bar{\hat{f}}_{l,k}(s,\xi)+\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\partial_{s}\bar{\hat{f}}_{l,k}(s,\xi)d\xi ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}2^{20k_{+}}\|\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\partial_{s}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{1}^{t}2^{20k_{+}-10k_{+}}\|\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{2}}{s^{1+\gamma/2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\|f_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([1,T])H^{10}_{x}}\int_{1}^{t}\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{2}}{s^{1+\gamma/2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\|f_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([1,T])H^{10}_{x}}\frac{\epsilon_{1}^{2}}{\gamma}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}+\epsilon_{1}^{3}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}^{2}.

Next, we use the result below whose proof is given in Section
6
.

Proposition 4.1
.

Under assumption (
1.8
),

\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}=\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}

(4.1)

for all
l
and
t\in[1,T]
.

Hence,

\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}.

Define

T^{+}=\sup\{T>1:\{u_{l}\}\text{ is a solution to }\eqref{pde}\text{ s.t. }\sup_{l}\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}\}.

Suppose
T^{+}<\infty
, then

\sup_{l}\sup_{t\in[1,T^{+}]}\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0}<\epsilon_{1},

given
\epsilon_{0}
sufficiently small. Hence, by local well-posedness, there exists some
T^{\prime}>T^{+}
such that
\{\tilde{u}_{l}\}
solves (
1.1
) and

\sup_{l}\sup_{t\in[1,T^{\prime}]}\|\tilde{u}_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}\tilde{u}_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1},

which gives a contradiction and shows
T^{+}=\infty
.

Thus, we can conclude

\sup_{t\in[1,\infty)}\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0},

(4.2)

which proves the existence of a global solution to the IVP (
1.1
) provided
\epsilon_{0}
sufficiently small. The uniqueness follows from the uniqueness for local well-posedness in Proposition
5.1
.

Furthermore, as a result of (
4.2
) above and (
3.10
), for any integer
M\geq 1
and
t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]

\displaystyle\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\|e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}f_{l,k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-2k_{+}+\gamma k+\gamma k/4-k},2^{\gamma k-2k_{+}+2k}\}\epsilon_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\leq-M/2}2^{\gamma k-2k_{+}+2k}\epsilon_{0}+\sum_{k>-M/2}2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-2k_{+}+\gamma k+\gamma k/4-k}\epsilon_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-M(1+\gamma/2)}\epsilon_{0}\lesssim t^{-(1+\gamma/2)}\epsilon_{0},

which proves (
1.7
).

Lastly, we show the scattering result. Since we now have

\sup_{t\in[1,\infty)}\|u_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}=\sup_{t\in[1,\infty)}\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0},

we know
\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-10k_{+}}t^{-1-\gamma/2}\epsilon_{0}^{2}
for all
t\geq 1
from Lemma
3.3
. Then for any
1\leq t_{1}<t_{2}
,

\displaystyle\|f_{l}(t_{2},x)-f_{l}(t_{1},x)\|^{2}_{H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\partial_{s}\|f_{l,k}(s,x)\|^{2}_{H^{10}_{x}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}2^{20k_{+}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{s}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\bar{\hat{f}}_{l,k}(s,\xi)+\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,\xi)\partial_{s}\bar{\hat{f}}_{l,k}(s,\xi)d\xi ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}2^{20k_{+}}\|\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{s}\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}2^{20k_{+}-10k_{+}}\|\hat{f}_{l,k}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}}\frac{\epsilon_{0}^{2}}{s^{1+\gamma/2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([1,\infty))H^{10}_{x}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\frac{\epsilon_{0}^{2}}{s^{1+\gamma/2}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\epsilon_{0}^{3}}{\gamma}(\frac{1}{t_{1}^{\gamma/2}}-\frac{1}{t_{2}^{\gamma/2}}).

Thus,
\tilde{f}_{n}(x)=f_{l}(n,x)
forms a Cauchy sequence in
H^{10}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
. Hence, there exists some
u^{*}(x)\in H^{10}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
so that
\|f_{l}(n,x)-u^{*}(x)\|_{H^{10}}\rightarrow 0
as
n\rightarrow\infty
. If
\lfloor t\rfloor
denote the integer part of
t
, then

\|f_{l}(t,x)-u^{*}(x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}\leq\|f_{l}(\lfloor t\rfloor,x)-u^{*}(x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|f_{l}(t,x)-f_{l}(\lfloor t\rfloor,x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}

implies
\|u_{l}(t,x)-e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}u^{*}(x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}=\|f_{l}(t,x)-u^{*}(x)\|_{H^{10}_{x}}\rightarrow 0
as
t\rightarrow\infty
.

5
Proof of Local Well-Posedness

The following local well-posedness result is essential for us to make the bootstrap assumption (
1.8
). The result indicates that if we start at any time
t_{0}>1
and have initial data
u_{l0}
with
e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}
bounded in the
H^{10}\cap Z
space, we can find a unique solution to the partial differential equation that exists on the time interval
[t_{0},T]
where
T
is only dependent on the starting time
t_{0}
and the size of our initial data. The proof uses Duhamel’s integral equation and is based on the contraction mapping principle.

Proposition 5.1

(local well-posedness)
.

Given
t_{0}\geq 1
. If
\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon
for some
\epsilon>0
, then there exists
T=T(t_{0},\epsilon)>t_{0}
so that the equation

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}u_{l}=ic_{l}\Delta u_{l}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}u_{m}u_{n}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}Q(u_{m},u_{n})\\
u_{l}|_{t=t_{0}}=u_{l0}\end{cases},

(5.1)

has a unique solution
u_{l}(t,x)
such that
e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)\in\mathcal{C}([t_{0},T];H^{10}\cap Z)
.

Proof.

Define an operator

\displaystyle\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}(t,x)=

\displaystyle e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}+\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\bigg)ds.

We shall show there exists some
T>t_{0}
and
r>0
such that for

\displaystyle X=\{\vec{f}\in\prod_{l}\mathcal{C}([t_{0},T];H^{10}\cap Z):\sup_{l}\sup_{t\in[t_{0},T]}\|f_{l}\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq r\},

\Psi:X\rightarrow X
, and furthermore,
\Psi
is a contraction map.

Take an arbitrary
\vec{f}\in X
and
t\in[t_{0},T]
. Our goal is to establish bounds for
\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{H^{10}_{x}}
and
\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{Z}
. These bounds will rely on the initial data
u_{0}
, the radius
r
, and the time interval
[t_{0},T]
. To proceed, we need to compute

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{H^{10}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle\|e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+(T-t_{0})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(T-t_{0})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+(T-t_{0})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|(1+|\xi|^{2})^{5}\mathcal{F}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad(T-t_{0})\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([1,T])H^{10}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}.

Using the
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate in Lemma
2.3
and Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
2.11
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|(1+|\xi|^{2})^{5}\mathcal{F}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\bigg\|\sum_{k,k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{10k_{+}}\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times e^{-ic_{m}s|\xi-\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)e^{-ic_{n}s|\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)d\eta\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k\leq\max\{k_{1},k_{2}\}+1}2^{10k_{+}}\psi_{k}(\xi)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times e^{-ic_{m}s|\xi-\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)e^{-ic_{n}s|\eta|^{2}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)d\eta\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k_{1}}\sum_{k\leq k_{1}}2^{10k_{+}+\epsilon k_{-}}\|e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k_{1}}2^{10k_{1,+}}\|e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\bigg\|\sum_{k_{1}\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k_{1}}2^{10k_{1,+}}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{10k_{2,+}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\|f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}.

Hence, there is some constant
C>0
independent of
f
so that

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}(T-t_{0})\|f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([1,T])H^{10}_{x}}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+C(T-t_{0})r^{2}.

Next, we move on to bounding the
Z
norm

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{Z}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|e^{-i\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{Z}+(T-t_{0})\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\bigg\|D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\mathcal{F}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\times

\displaystyle\times\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\bigg)\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}},

where according to the computation on page
3.2
and (
3.17
),

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\bigg)\psi_{k}(\xi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}\in\mathbb{Z}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(x,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

for
\phi(\xi,\eta)=c_{l}|\xi|^{2}-c_{m}|\xi-\eta|^{2}-c_{n}|\eta|^{2}
.
Since
D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}=D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\nabla_{\xi}
, we look at the first derivative in
\xi
for each term above,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{l}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.2)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.3)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.4)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{l}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.5)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.6)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(5.7)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta.

(5.8)

Split the terms in the first derivative according to the number of derivatives on
f_{m,k_{1}}
and define

F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\eqref{lwp1.1}+\eqref{lwp1.3}+\eqref{lwp2.1}+\eqref{lwp2.3-1}+\eqref{lwp2.3},

F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\eqref{lwp1.2}+\eqref{lwp2.2}.

From condition (
1.2
) on the multiplier
q
, Lemma
2.6
, and Lemma
2.7
, we know

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{k}+2^{k_{2}}\lesssim 2^{k_{1}}

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k}.

Then, by Lemma
2.4
,

\displaystyle\|F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k}+\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

(5.9)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k})\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

and

\displaystyle\|F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

(5.10)

In order to bound the
1+\alpha
derivatives, we take another derivative in
\xi
and get

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}[\eqref{lwp1.1}+\eqref{lwp1.3}]=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}s^{2}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{lwp1.2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}[\eqref{lwp2.1}+\eqref{lwp2.3-1}+\eqref{lwp2.3}]=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}s^{2}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{lwp2.2}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}is\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta.

Using Lemma
2.6
, Lemma
2.7
, and (
1.2
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{2k_{1}}

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla^{2}_{\xi}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k}.

Hence, employing Lemma
2.4
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(T+T^{2}2^{2k_{1}}+T2^{k_{1}}\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}+T2^{k_{1}+\epsilon k_{-}-k}+\|\nabla^{2}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k}+

(5.11)

\displaystyle+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k}\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}})\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+(T2^{k_{1}}+\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k})\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T+T^{2}2^{2k_{1}}+T2^{k_{1}-k}+2^{-2k})\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k})\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k})\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

(5.12)

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k})\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle T2^{k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+(1+2^{-k+k_{1}})\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k+k_{1}}+1)\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

since (
2.6
) implies
\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{k_{1}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}
.

Using Lemma
3.1
,
f\in X
implies for any
t\in[t_{0},T]

\displaystyle\|\hat{f}_{l,k}\|_{H^{1}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2}r,\,\|\hat{f}_{l,k}\|_{H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}r,

(5.13)

and (
3.6
), (
3.8
) implies
\|f_{l,k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\min\{2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k+k/2},2^{-10k_{+}}\}r.

Thus, as a result of Lemma
2.1
,

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\leq\|F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{\alpha},

and using (
5.9
) and (
5.11
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k}+T+T^{2}2^{2k_{1}}+T2^{k_{1}-k}+2^{-2k})\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}_{x}}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}(T2^{k}+2^{-k}+T+T^{2}2^{2k}+2^{-2k})\min\{2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k+k/2-\alpha k},2^{-6k_{+}}\}r2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}(T2^{2k}+1+T2^{k}+T^{2}2^{3k}+2^{-k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}\min\{1,2^{-4k_{+}-\gamma k-k/2+\alpha k}\}r2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}(1+T2^{k}+T2^{2k}+T^{2}2^{3k})\min\{1,2^{-4k_{+}-\gamma k-k/2+\alpha k}\}r\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}2^{10k_{2,+}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}T^{2}r\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}F^{0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}(T2^{k_{2}}+2^{-k}+T+T^{2}2^{2k_{2}}+T2^{k_{2}-k}+2^{-2k})\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\|f_{m,k_{1}}(s,x)\|^{1-\alpha}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|^{\alpha}_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{3k/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{2}\geq k-2a-2}(T2^{k_{2}}+2^{-k}+T^{2}2^{2k_{2}}+T2^{k_{2}-k}+2^{-2k})2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2-\alpha k_{2}}r2^{3k/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-k/2}\sum_{k_{2}\geq k-2a-2}(T2^{3k_{2}/2+2k}+2^{k+k_{2}/2}+T^{2}2^{5k_{2}/2+2k}+T2^{3k_{2}/2+k}+2^{k_{2}/2})2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k_{2}}r\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}T^{2}r\sum_{k_{2}\geq k-2a-2}2^{10k_{2,+}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}T^{2}r\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}.

For the estimation of
F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we will use the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
. We can define a family of operators on the strip
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}(s,\xi)=D^{z}_{\xi}\bigg(

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta\bigg).

From (
5.10
) and (
5.12
), we know

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\hat{g}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{1}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

and

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k+k_{1}}+1)\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k+k_{1}}+1)\|\hat{g}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.

Thus, by the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
,

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}=

\displaystyle\|T_{\alpha}(\hat{f}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}})(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(T2^{k_{1}}+2^{-k+k_{1}}+1)\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}},

and (
5.13
) implies for any
s\in[t_{0},T]

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}(T2^{k_{2}}+2^{-k+k_{1}}+1)2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}}r2^{3k/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}\sum_{k_{2}\geq k-2a-2}(T2^{k_{2}+3k/2}+2^{k_{2}+k/2})2^{(\gamma-1/2-\alpha)(k_{2}-k)}r\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}Tr\sum_{k_{2}\geq k-2a-2}2^{10k_{2,+}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}Tr\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}.

For
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
, we need a new bound for
\|\nabla F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}
. Using the identity
\sum_{j}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{lwp1.2}=-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

and

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{lwp2.2}=

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{is\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta.

Since (
3.22
) implies

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}}

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 1,

and
\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{n}\xi
when
c_{n}+c_{m}=0
implies

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k_{2}}

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}},

we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{-k_{2}})\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{k_{2}})\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{2}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Thus, by the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
,

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{k_{2}})\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}^{1-\alpha}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{1}_{\xi}}^{\alpha},

and (
3.3
), (
5.13
) imply that for any
s\in[t_{0},T]

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}F^{1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}(1+2^{k_{2}})2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}}r2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{k_{2}}(s,x)\|^{1/2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(s,\xi)\|_{H^{1}_{\xi}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}r\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}(1+2^{k_{2}})2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|^{1/2}_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{-k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/4}r^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}r^{3/2}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}2^{5k_{2,+}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}(s,x)\|^{1/2}_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}r^{3/2}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}^{1/2}.

Summarizing the results above, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\mathcal{F}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\big)\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}Tr\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}+2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}r^{3/2}\|f_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}Tr^{2}.

Hence, there exists some constant
C^{\prime}>0
independent of
f
so that

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{Z}\leq

\displaystyle\|e^{-ic_{l}\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{Z}+C^{\prime}(T-t_{0})Tr^{2}.

Since
\|u_{l0}\|_{H^{10}}+\|e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon
, we can pick
r=2\epsilon
and
T=T(\epsilon,t_{0})>t_{0}
so that
\max\{C,C^{\prime}\}(T-t_{0})Tr<1/12
. Then

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{H^{10}_{x}}+\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon+C(T-t_{0})r^{2}+C^{\prime}(T-t_{0})Tr^{2}\leq\epsilon+r/12+r/12\leq r.

Thus, we proved
\Psi:X\rightarrow X
.

Following along the same lines from page
5
to page
5
, we have for arbitrary
\vec{f},\vec{g}\in X

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle C\|f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}\|g_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\vec{f}\|_{X}\|\vec{g}\|_{X}

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\mathcal{F}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n})\big)\psi_{k}(\xi)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C^{\prime}2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}T(\|f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])Z}+\|f_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}})(\|g_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])Z}+\|g_{n}\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C^{\prime}2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}T\|\vec{f}\|_{X}\|\vec{g}\|_{X},

which imply

\displaystyle\|e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n})+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n})\big)\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])Z}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C^{\prime}T\|\vec{f}\|_{X}\|\vec{g}\|_{X}.

Now, we are ready to show
\Psi
is a contraction map. Suppose
\vec{f},\vec{g}\in X
, then

\displaystyle\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}-\Psi(\vec{g})_{l}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n}))+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}((e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n}))\bigg)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n}))+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}((e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})-(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}g_{n}))\bigg)ds

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}),e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n}))+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}((e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}))(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n})))\bigg)ds.

For any
t\in[t_{0},T]
,

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}),e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n}))\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\|(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}))(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n})\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}\|_{X}+C\|\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}),e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}(f_{m}-g_{m}))(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\big)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])Z}+

\displaystyle+\bigg\|e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}g_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}(f_{n}-g_{n}))\big)\bigg\|_{L^{\infty}_{s}([t_{0},T])Z}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C^{\prime}T\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}\|_{X}+C^{\prime}T\|\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}.

Since
\vec{f},\vec{g}\in X
, we know
\|\vec{f}\|_{X}+\|\vec{g}\|_{X}\leq 2r
. Thus,

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})_{l}-\Psi(\vec{g})_{l}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])H^{10}_{x}}\leq

\displaystyle 2(T-t_{0})C(\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}\|_{X}+\|\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4r(T-t_{0})C\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}

and

\displaystyle\|\Psi(f)-\Psi(g)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([t_{0},T])Z}\leq

\displaystyle(T-t_{0})(C^{\prime}T\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}\|_{X}+C^{\prime}T\|\vec{g}\|_{X}\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2rC^{\prime}T(T-t_{0})\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}.

Since we required
\max\{C,C^{\prime}\}(T-t_{0})Tr<1/12
,

\displaystyle\|\Psi(\vec{f})-\Psi(\vec{g})\|_{X}\leq

\displaystyle 4r(T-t_{0})C\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}+2rC^{\prime}T(T-t_{0})\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(4r(T-t_{0})C+2rC^{\prime}T(T-t_{0}))\|\vec{f}-\vec{g}\|_{X}

with
4r(T-t_{0})C+2rC^{\prime}T(T-t_{0})<1
. This shows that
\Psi:X\rightarrow X
is a contraction map. Therefore, by the contraction mapping principle
\Psi
has a unique fixed point
\vec{f}\in X
such that

\displaystyle f_{l}(t,x)=

\displaystyle e^{-ic_{l}t_{0}\Delta}u_{l0}+\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{-ic_{l}s\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\bigg)ds.

Let
u_{l}(t,x)=e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}f_{l}(t,x)
and observe that

\displaystyle u_{l}(t,x)=e^{ic_{l}t\Delta}f_{l}(t,x)

\displaystyle=e^{ic_{l}(t-t_{0})\Delta}u_{l0}+\int_{t_{0}}^{t}e^{ic_{l}(t-s)\Delta}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}Q(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m},e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}(e^{ic_{m}s\Delta}f_{m})(e^{ic_{n}s\Delta}f_{n})\bigg)ds,

solves (
5.1
) according to Duhamel’s principle.
∎

6
Proof of Proposition
4.1

In this section, we prove the main proposition stated in Section
4
. This is the crucial result that shows the
Z
norm of the profile
f_{l}(t,x)=e^{-ic_{l}t\Delta}u_{l}(t,x)
remains bounded independently of the growth of the time variable, which leads to global well-posedness. The main goal of the proof is to deal with the additional powers of
t
introduced by the
1+\alpha
derivatives in the
Z
norm.

Similarly to what we did in the proof of Proposition
5.1
and Lemma
3.3
, we will employ the integral equation and use the Littlewood-Paley operator to further decompose the frequency spaces of profiles
f_{m},f_{n}
in the nonlinear term.

Recall the definition of
\|\cdot\|_{Z}
in (
1.3
). We decompose the time interval
[1,T]
into dyadic intervals and observe

\displaystyle\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u_{l0}\|_{Z}+\big\|\int_{1}^{t}\partial_{s}f_{l}(s,x)ds\big\|_{Z}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}+\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}\|f_{l}(t_{2},x)-f_{l}(t_{1},x)\|_{Z}

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon_{0}+\sup_{k\in\mathbb{Z}}\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\|D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{2},\xi)-D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{1},\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

To prove

\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\lesssim\epsilon_{0},

for all
t\in[1,T]
, it suffices to show for all
k\in\mathbb{Z}
,

\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\|D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{2},\xi)-D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{1},\xi)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2},

since
\epsilon_{1}^{2}=\epsilon_{0}^{10/6}\leq\epsilon_{0}
.

For
2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}
, let

G^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

and

G^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

Then we have

\displaystyle\psi_{k}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}G^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}G^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

(6.1)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}G^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}G^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}.

Taking one derivative on
G^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
G^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
results in the terms
I^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
J^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
,

\displaystyle I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.2)

I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.3)

I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.4)

\displaystyle J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.5)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

\displaystyle J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.6)

\displaystyle J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.7)

Since

\displaystyle D^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}[\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{2},\xi)-\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{1},\xi)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\nabla_{\xi}[\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{2},\xi)-\hat{f}_{l,k}(t_{1},\xi)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}}\sum_{i=0,1,2}D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}}\sum_{i=0,1,2}D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\sum_{i=0,1,2}D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
m\geq n\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\sum_{i=0,1,2}D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}},

the problem is now reduced to estimating the
\alpha
derivative of
I^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
J^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, where
i=0,1,2
.

We shall use the interpolation results developed in Section
2.1
to tackle the estimation for the
\alpha
derivative of the terms above. We will discuss the terms
D^{\alpha}I^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
D^{\alpha}J^{M,i}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
for
i=0,1,2
in Sections
6.1
,
6.2
,
6.3
respectively.

6.1
D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
D^{\alpha}J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

In this section, we will show

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+

(6.8)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}

In order to gain decay in
t
, we want to use the following identity

e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}

for integration by parts. Bounding the right hand side requires the norm of
\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{m}(\xi-\eta)-2c_{n}\eta
to have a lower bound. However, we can only achieve a lower bound when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
for a general pair of
c_{m}
and
c_{n}
. When
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
, we observe
\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{m}\xi
, whose norm is bounded from below due to the localization.

Thus, we will discuss the bounds in two different cases. The bound for
\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}
when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
will be dealt with in Lemma
6.2
, while the lemma below covers all the other terms.

Lemma 6.1
.

Given
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}(t,x)\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{-M-k-k_{1}})2^{-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-\alpha k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

when
c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0
and
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}
, and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-\alpha k-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

when
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
and
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}
.

Proof.

Consider the following families of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

\displaystyle T^{1}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

and

\displaystyle T^{2}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

Since we want to estimate the
\alpha
derivative, we may employ the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
. The first operator
T_{z}
is used to estimate
I_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}^{M,0}
, while
T_{z}^{1}
and
T^{2}_{z}
are defined to handle
J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.
In this proof, we will show
T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
,
T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
,
T^{1}_{0+iy},T^{2}_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
,
T^{1}_{1+iy},T^{2}_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
are bounded. Then performing interpolation will give the desired estimates on the
\alpha
derivative in the lemma.

We start with obtaining bounds for the operators when
\Re(z)=0
. By the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
in Lemma
2.3
, Lemma
2.4
, (
3.13
), (
3.2
), and
(
3.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigg\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/2+k_{1}/4+k_{2}/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

Using the same method as above with (
1.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

Next, we look at the operators when
\Re(z)=1
,

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.9)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.10)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}},

(6.11)

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.12)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.13)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.14)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}},

(6.15)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.16)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.17)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.18)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.19)

Since
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
, we have (
3.22
) for
c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0
and are able to perform an integration by parts on
(
6.10
)
and
(
6.11
)
using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
,

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.20)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.21)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.22)

and

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.23)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.24)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.25)

For
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
, recall that
\phi(\xi,\eta)=(c_{l}+c_{n})|\xi|^{2}-2c_{n}\xi\cdot\eta
. We thus integrate by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\xi_{j}}{it2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the terms
(
6.15
)
and
(
6.19
)
,

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.26)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big]\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

and

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}\}.

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/2-k_{2}/2-\alpha k_{1}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{2}/2+3k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-\alpha k-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

where the bounds on the Sobolev norms are given in (
3.3
).
∎

Thus, as a result of Lemma
6.1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}}(1+2^{-M-k-k_{1}})2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}-k/2+\alpha(k-k_{1})-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{\epsilon k_{-}-\gamma k+2k_{+}-k/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\bigg(\sum_{k_{2}\leq k-2a-2}(1+2^{-M-2k})2^{-k/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3k_{2}/2+3k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\leq k_{2}+2a+2}(1+2^{-M-k-k_{2}})2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}-k/2+\alpha(k-k_{2})-4k_{2,+}+2\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3k/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\leq k_{2}+2a+2}2^{\epsilon k_{-}-\gamma k+2k_{+}-k/2-4k_{2,+}+2\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/2}\min\{2^{-M/2},2^{M+3k/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(6.32)

We are left to show

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

For
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{3}=\{|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq a,|k-k_{1}|\leq a+2\}
, we observe

|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|=|2c_{m}\xi-2(c_{m}+c_{n})\eta|=0

if
c_{m}\xi=(c_{m}+c_{n})\eta
. Hence, in the proof above, integration by parts method using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
cannot be applied to estimate
(
6.10
)
and
(
6.11
)
. Instead, we will take advantage of the fact that
\chi^{3}_{k}
is a finite set with uniformly bounded cardinality for all
k
. In Lemma
6.2
, we estimate the
\alpha
derivative using the interpolation result in Lemma
2.1
.

Lemma 6.2
.

Given
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
, and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+3k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

when
M\leq-2k
, and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M/2-3k/2+\alpha M+\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

when
M>-2k
.

Proof.

Since Lemma
2.1
implies

\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha},

we shall find bounds for
\|I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}
and
\|\nabla I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}
when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
.

First, using the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
in Lemma
2.3
, together with Lemma
2.4
, (
3.13
) and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\|I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\bigg\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{4}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-3k/2},2^{M+3k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

given that
k,k_{1},k_{2}
are about the same size when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
.

Next, we look at the first-order derivative,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.33)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.34)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.35)

By the bilinear estimate
L^{18/7}\times L^{9}\rightarrow L^{2}
, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.13
, (
3.2
) and (
3.7
)

\displaystyle\|\nabla I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im0-2.1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im0-2.12}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im0-2.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/7}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle+\big(2^{M}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+2^{2m}\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{18/7}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/2}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/11}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9/8}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\}

\displaystyle+(2^{M-2k}+2^{2M-k+k_{2}})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/11}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9/8}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-5k_{1}/6-2k_{2}/3},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+2^{M+k+k_{2}})2^{M-2k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+k_{1}/6-2k_{2}/3},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M+2k})2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-5k/2},2^{M+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we have when
M\leq-2k
,
