\displaystyle\|I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+3k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

so Lemma
2.1
implies

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+3k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

In the other case when
M>-2k
,

\displaystyle\|I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M/2-3k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M/2-k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Then, applying Lemma
2.1
gives

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M/2-3k/2+\alpha M+\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

∎

Therefore, Lemma
6.2
implies for any
k\in\mathbb{Z}

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,0}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq-2k}2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k+M+2k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{-2k<M\leq\log T}2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M/2-k+\alpha M+2\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2},

since
\alpha<1/2
. This result combined with (
6.1
) gives (
6.8
).

6.2
D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
,
D^{\alpha}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

In this section, we will show

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+

(6.36)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq m\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}<t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\epsilon_{1}^{2}

When
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
, i.e.
|\xi|\sim|\xi-\eta|\geq 2^{a-2}|\eta|
, we want to integrate by parts in the time variable using the identity

e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}.

Recall that we defined
\phi(\xi,\eta)=c_{l}|\xi|^{2}-c_{m}|\xi-\eta|^{2}-c_{n}|\eta|^{2}
. There is no guaranteed lower bound on
|\phi(\xi,\eta)|
when
c_{l}=c_{m}
. Hence, in Lemma
6.3
, we only look at
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
excluding
c_{l}=c_{m}
. The special case of
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
will be tackled later in Lemma
6.4
.

Now we show the lower bound on
|\phi(\xi,\eta)|
that we will use when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
. Recall the definition of
a
in (
3.16
), we have

\displaystyle|\phi(\xi,\eta)|

\displaystyle=\big|(c_{l}-c_{m})|\xi-\eta|^{2}+2c_{l}\xi\cdot\eta-(c_{l}+c_{n})|\eta|^{2}\big|

(6.37)

\displaystyle=|c_{l}-c_{m}|\big||\xi-\eta|^{2}+\frac{2c_{l}}{c_{l}-c_{m}}\xi\cdot\eta-\frac{c_{l}+c_{n}}{c_{l}-c_{m}}|\eta|^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\geq|c_{l}-c_{m}|\big(2^{2k_{1}-4}-\frac{2}{|1-c_{m}/c_{l}|}2^{k+k_{2}}-\frac{1}{|1-c_{m}/c_{l}|}2^{2k_{2}}-\frac{|c_{n}|}{|c_{l}-c_{m}|}2^{2k_{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\geq|c_{l}-c_{m}|\big(2^{2k_{1}-4}-\frac{1}{|1-c_{m}/c_{l}|}2^{2k_{1}+4-a}-\frac{1}{|1-c_{m}/c_{l}|}2^{2k_{1}-2a}-\frac{|c_{n}|}{|c_{l}-c_{m}|}2^{2k_{1}-2a}\big)

\displaystyle\geq|c_{l}-c_{m}|\big(2^{2k_{1}-4}-2^{2k_{1}-6}-2^{2k_{1}-10}-2^{2k_{1}-6}\big)

\displaystyle\geq|c_{l}-c_{m}|\big(2^{2k_{1}-5}-2^{2k_{1}-10}\big)\gtrsim 2^{2k_{1}}.

In the proof for Lemma
6.3
, we will employ the bound above with Lemma
2.1
to obtain the desired estimates.

Lemma 6.3
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. For
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-k-\alpha k-k_{2}/2},2^{M+2k_{2}-k/2-\alpha k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

if
M+2k\leq 0
, and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+\gamma k/2-2k+\alpha k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k+3k_{2}/2+\alpha k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

if
M+2k>0
.

Proof.

Recall the definition of
I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
in (
6.3
) and (
6.6
). Define

\displaystyle F^{1}(\xi)=I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

and

\displaystyle F^{2}(\xi)=J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

By Lemma
2.1
, we get

\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}=\|D^{\alpha}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}

and

\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}=\|D^{\alpha}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}.

We shall find
L^{2}
bounds on
F^{1}
,
F^{2}
and their derivatives.

First, by the bilinear estimate
L^{3}\times L^{6}\rightarrow L^{2}
in Lemma
2.3
, Lemma
2.4
, and (
1.2
),

\displaystyle\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}(1+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}})\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}.

Using Lemma
2.13
, (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
3.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

(6.38)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+2k_{2}-k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

However, this bound on
\|F^{1}+F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}
is not small enough when
M+2k>0
. In this case,
we integrate by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
to obtain another
L^{2}
bound,

\displaystyle F^{1}(\xi)+F^{2}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{2},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{2},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.39)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{1}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{1},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{1},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.40)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{t}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.41)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.42)

Since
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
,

\big|\phi(\xi,\eta)\big|=\big|c_{l}|\xi|^{2}-c_{m}|\xi-\eta|^{2}-c_{n}|\eta|^{2}\big|\gtrsim 2^{2k_{1}},

ideally, we shall gain a factor of

t^{-1}|\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{-1}\sim 2^{-M-2k_{1}}

through integration by parts. But given that there are only
1+\alpha
derivatives of
\hat{f}_{k}
in the
Z
norm for us to work with, we cannot quite achieve
2^{-M-2k_{1}}
. This is shown in the estimations below.

By the
L^{3}\times L^{6}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.13
, along with (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
3.12
),

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.b1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm1.b2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{1}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-M-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4-k_{2}/2},2^{2k_{2}-k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.4}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm1.5}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{1}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{t}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{t}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big).

Then, using the bounds on the time derivatives in Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
3.4
, and Lemma
3.5
, we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.4}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm1.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-M-\gamma k_{1}-M-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k_{1}})2^{3k_{2}/2-M-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-2M-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2-M+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-\gamma k_{-}-k-k_{2}/2},2^{-k/2-\gamma k_{-}+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we conclude

\displaystyle\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k-k_{2}/2},2^{-k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(6.43)

Now, we deal with the terms
\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}
,
\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}
. Since we will not be able to bound
\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}}
, before taking the derivative in
\xi
, we perform a change of variable
\zeta=\xi-\eta
on
F^{1}+F^{2}
. As a consequence, we will have the two derivatives evenly distributed between
\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}
and
\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}
,

\displaystyle F^{1}+F^{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}(1+q(\eta,\xi-\eta))\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\zeta)}(1+q(\xi-\zeta,\zeta))\partial_{\zeta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\zeta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\zeta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\zeta dt.

Hence,

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}F^{1}+\partial_{\xi_{m}}F^{2}

(6.44)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.45)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.46)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.47)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.48)

For the term
(
6.48
)
, after integrating by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}=\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}
, we get

\displaystyle\eqref{jm1.2}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{2}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{t_{2}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{2},\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{2},\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.49)

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{1}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{t_{1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{1},\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{1},\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.50)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.51)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{t}\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.52)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.53)

Using the
L^{3}\times L^{6}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate and Lemma
2.4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

Then, by Bernstein’s inequality, (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
3.12
),

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M+\gamma M/4+\gamma k_{1}/4-k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{\gamma M/4+\gamma k/4-k+\gamma k_{2}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next, the bilinear estimate
L^{3}\times L^{6}\rightarrow L^{2}
, together with Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.13
, (
3.2
), and (
3.12
) implies

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.11}+\eqref{jm1.12}+\eqref{jm1.b1-2}+\eqref{jm1.b2-2}+\eqref{jm1.3-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-2k+\gamma k/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+2k_{2}-3k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.4-2}+\eqref{jm1.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}(1+q(\xi-\eta,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{t}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{t}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{t}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big),

and by Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
3.4
, Lemma
3.5
, (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
3.12
),

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm1.4-2}+\eqref{jm1.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-M-\gamma k_{1}-M-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k_{1}})2^{3k_{2}/2-M-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-2M-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2-M+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-k-\gamma k-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{M-3k/2-\gamma k+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-2k+\gamma k/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M-3k/2+2k_{2}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-3k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{(1+2^{\gamma M/4+\gamma k/2})2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

When
M+2k\leq 0
, using Lemma
2.1
and (
6.38
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-k-\alpha k-k_{2}/2},2^{M+2k_{2}-k/2-\alpha k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

And when
M+2k>0
, we use Lemma
2.1
and (
6.43
) to get

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}F^{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}F^{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M+\alpha M+\gamma M/4+\gamma k/2-2k+\alpha k-k_{2}/2},2^{M/2+\alpha M-3k/2+2k_{2}+\alpha(2k-k_{2})}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+\gamma k/2-2k+\alpha k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k+3k_{2}/2+\alpha k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

The last line uses the fact that
\alpha(k-k_{2})\leq k/2-k_{2}/2
, since
k>k_{2}
for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
\alpha<1/2
.
∎

Since the denominator in the identity

e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}

has a lower bound,
|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim 2^{k_{1}}
, for all
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
, we will employ it to handle the general case when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
and the exception case in Lemma
6.3
when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
.

Lemma 6.4
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k-k_{1}/2}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k-k_{1}/2}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

We will use the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
to find bounds on the fractional derivatives. For
I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we consider the families of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

and

\displaystyle T^{q}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

respectively. We shall show for each
y\in\mathbb{R}
,

T_{0+iy},T^{q}_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

and

T_{1+iy},T^{q}_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

are bounded operators.

By Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
3.1
, and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{q}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\epsilon k_{-}})\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Next, we observe

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{l}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.54)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.55)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.56)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{q}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{l}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.57)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{l}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.58)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.59)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.60)

Since we have (
3.22
) when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
, we can perform an integration by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the term
(
6.56
)
,

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it2\eta\cdot(\xi-\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.61)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it2\eta\cdot(\xi-\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.62)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it2\eta\cdot(\xi-\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.63)

Furthermore, for
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
, we get
\phi(\xi,\eta)=(c_{l}-c_{m})|\xi|^{2}+2c_{m}\xi\cdot\eta
and
\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{m}\xi
. We may perform an integration by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{n=1}^{3}\frac{\xi_{n}}{it2c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the term
(
6.60
)
regardless of
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
,

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\big(\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.64)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.65)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.66)

By Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
2.7
, (
3.2
), and (
2.6
), we know

\displaystyle\eqref{stein1.1}+\|\eqref{stein1.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(1+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\eqref{stein1.12}+\|\eqref{stein1.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{-k}+2^{-k_{1}})\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

When
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
, we have
c_{l}=c_{m}
and
\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{m}\xi\cdot\eta-(c_{m}+c_{n})|\eta|^{2}
, so

\displaystyle\|\eqref{stein1.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{m+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Repeat the same estimate using (
1.2
). Since either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
\phi(\xi,\eta)=2c_{m}\xi\cdot\eta-(c_{m}+c_{n})|\eta|^{2}
, we get

\displaystyle\eqref{kstein1.1}+\|\eqref{kstein1.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{\epsilon k_{-}}+2^{\epsilon k_{-}+k_{2}-k})2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\eqref{kstein1.11}+\eqref{kstein1.12}+\|\eqref{kstein1.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\big(\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{kstein1.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{n}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{c_{m}|\xi|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{m+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Thus, we can conclude

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}-k+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{1}-k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\|T^{q}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\|T^{q}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{1}-k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

By the variation of the Stein’s interpolation theorem in Lemma
2.2
, for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\|T_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{q}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-m/4-k_{2}/2},2^{m+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

Taking
g=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m}(t,\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi)
and using (
3.3
) finish the proof.
∎

Now, the only case left is
\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}
for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
. We will start with proving a slightly more general result on the bilinear integrals when
|\xi|\sim|\eta|\sim|\xi-\eta|
. The proof will use Lemma
2.2
to handle the fractional derivatives.

Lemma 6.5
.

Suppose
m_{d}:\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
is homogeneous of degree
d
and smooth on
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus\{(0,0)\}
. Given
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.67)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.68)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}},

for any
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
.

Proof.

Consider the following two families of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T^{1}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

and

\displaystyle T^{2}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

which correspond to (
6.67
) and (
6.68
).

We start with bounding
T^{1}_{0+iy}
and
T^{2}_{0+iy}
.
Using the bilinear estimate
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
, Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, (
3.2
), and (
3.10
), we get

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\|g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}},

since
\chi^{3}_{k}=\{|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq a,|k-k_{1}|\leq a+2\}
. Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\|g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}.

Next, we observe

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.69)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.70)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.71)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.72)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.73)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.74)

By the
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, (
3.2
), (
2.6
), (
3.7
), and (
3.11
), we get

\displaystyle\eqref{chi3*.1}+\eqref{chi3*.1-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{-M/2+3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+

\displaystyle+\min\{\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{-M/2+3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{2^{-M/2+3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+

\displaystyle+\min\{\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}2^{-M/2+3k_{2}/2},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+dk}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{2}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+

\displaystyle+2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/2+3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}-k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{M/2+k},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{m-2},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

and the
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.4
, (
2.6
), and (
3.13
) imply

\displaystyle\eqref{chi3*.12}+\eqref{chi3*.2}+\eqref{chi3*.12-2}+\eqref{chi3*.2-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{M-k+dk}+2^{2M+dk+k})\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}g_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{M-k+dk}+2^{2M+dk+k})\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4+k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}+k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+k_{1}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M+2k})2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

When
M\leq-2k
, we have
2^{M+2k}\leq 1
. Thus,

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

By the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
, for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
, we get

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

In the other case when
M>-2k
, we have

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M+2k+dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Lemma
2.2
implies that for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\alpha(M+2k)+dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}=\|T^{1}_{\alpha}(\hat{g}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}m_{d}(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}=\|T^{2}_{\alpha}(\hat{g}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{dk-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

∎

Observe
\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}
for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
is special case of (
6.67
) in Lemma
6.5
with
\hat{g}=\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m}
,
d=0
. Employing (
3.3
), we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{M\leq-2k}2^{\gamma k+M+2k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{-2k<M\leq\log T}2^{\alpha(M+2k)-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2},

given
\alpha+(1-\alpha)\gamma/8<1/2
, i.e.
\alpha<1/2-\gamma/(16-2\gamma)
.

Combining this with the result in Lemma
6.3
and Lemma
6.4
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\bigg(\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
c_{m}\neq c_{l}\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
c_{m}=c_{l}\end{subarray}}\big)A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
c_{m}\neq c_{l}\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
c_{m}=c_{l}\end{subarray}}\big)A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,1}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{-\gamma k+2k_{+}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k/2-k_{1}/2}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq M\leq\min\{\log T,-2k\}}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}2^{-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-k/2-k_{2}/2},2^{M+2k_{2}+\alpha k-\alpha k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{-2k\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{k_{2}\leq k}2^{-\gamma k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+2\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+\gamma k/2-3k/2-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2},

which proves (
6.36
).

6.3
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}

In the current section, we examine the remaining terms, (
6.6
) and (
6.7
).

For
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we handle the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{m}\neq c_{l}
in Section
6.3.3
. In Section
6.3.1
, we further investigate the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
, but with the coefficients
c_{m}=c_{l}
, or the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
. Lastly, the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
are addressed in Section
6.3.4
.

As for
D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we look at the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{m}\neq c_{l}
in Section
6.3.3
, while all the remaining cases,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{m}=c_{l}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}
, are estimated in Section
6.3.2
.

6.3.1
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}

Recall the lower bound
|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim 2^{k_{1}}
established in
(
3.22
)
, when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}\cup\chi^{2}_{k}
. We will frequently employ the following identity throughout this section,

\sum_{m}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}.

To estimate the
\alpha
derivative, we will utilize the interpolation result from Lemma
2.2
. However, instead of immediately defining a single family of operators for
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
to apply the lemma to, we will take a preliminary step. We will use the aforementioned identity to expand and split
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
in the following way,

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.75)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.76)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.77)

This procedure adds flexibility to our estimation by allowing us to deal with each term separately using Lemma
2.2
.

Lemma 6.6
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2.1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Consider the following family of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

First, we want to show the operator
T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
is bounded for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
.

The Strichartz’s estimate in Lemma
2.10
, together with Lemma
2.7
, Lemma
2.4
, (
3.3
), (
3.2
), and (
2.6
), provides

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Then, we need to prove for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
, the operator
T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
is bounded.

Observe that

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{n}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.78)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.79)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.80)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}},

(6.81)

and perform integration by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the term
(
6.81
)
,

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.82)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.83)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.84)

Using the Strichartz’s estimate in Lemma
2.10
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.7
, as well as estimations (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\eqref{im2-1.1}+\|\eqref{im2-1.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\eqref{im2-1.11}+\eqref{im2-1.12}+\|\eqref{im2-1.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

In addition, since we either have
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2-1.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-2k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-2k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-2k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Thus, we found

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{1}-k+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+2k_{1}-k+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

By Lemma
2.2
, for any
\alpha\in(0,1)

\displaystyle\|T_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}.

As we plug in
\mathcal{F}^{-1}\hat{f}_{m}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}
for
g
in the result above, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2.1}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{m+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-3k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

using (
3.3
).
∎

Next, we turn to the estimation for the second term.

Lemma 6.7
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{m}=c_{l}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2.3}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-k_{1}/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Consider the following family of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

We want to show that for any
y\in\mathbb{R}
, the operators
T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
and
T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
are bounded.

Employing the Strichartz’s estimate in Lemma
2.10
, along with Lemma
2.7
, Lemma
2.4
, and using (
3.3
), (
3.2
), and (
2.6
), we get

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Next, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq
