\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{n}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.85)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.86)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.87)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.88)

Integration by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the term
(
6.88
)
gives

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\big)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.89)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.90)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.91)

Using the Strichartz’s estimate in Lemma
2.10
, together with Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.7
, (
3.3
), (
3.2
), (
3.7
), and (
2.6
), we obtain

\displaystyle\eqref{im2-3.1}+\|\eqref{im2-3.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{k_{2}-k_{1}}+2^{2k_{2}-2k_{1}})\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\eqref{im2-3.11}+\eqref{im2-3.12}+\|\eqref{im2-3.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{k_{2}-2k_{1}}+2^{-k+k_{2}-k_{1}}+2^{k_{2}-2k_{1}})\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k+k_{2}-k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{1}}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2-3.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{j}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2k_{2}-2k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2k_{2}-2k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}-k_{2}/2}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Thus, combining the estimates above, we found

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|T_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}-k+3k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle+2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-2k_{1}+3k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

By the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
.

When we take
\mathcal{F}^{-1}\hat{f}_{m}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}
for
g
and use (
3.3
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}/2-\alpha k_{1}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k_{1}/2-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

as desired.
∎

Now, we turn to the final term in the splitting of
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

Lemma 6.8
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f_{l}\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{m}=c_{l}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-\alpha k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/4+k_{2}/2-3k_{1}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

We start with defining the following family of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

which we want to show
T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
and
T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}
are bounded for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
.

Based on the Strichartz’s estimate in Lemma
2.7
, as well as Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.10
, (
2.6
), (
3.2
), and (
3.3
), we can get

\displaystyle\|T_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta))\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{2^{-M/4}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k_{2}-k_{1}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/4-k_{1}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Lemma 6.9
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2.1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k_{2}/4-k_{1}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

First, we define two families of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T^{1}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

and

\displaystyle T^{2}_{z}\hat{g}=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

for (
6.100
) and (
6.101
) respectively.
We want to find bounds on the operators

T^{1}_{0+iy},T^{2}_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

and

T^{1}_{1+iy},T^{2}_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
.

Beginning with
T^{1}_{0+iy}
and
T^{2}_{0+iy}
, we can employ the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
, together with (
3.13
) and (
1.2
) to get

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle+2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2+k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Combining this result with Lemma
2.4
and (
3.2
) leads to

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Next, for
T^{1}_{1+iy}
and
T^{2}_{1+iy}
, we compute

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{n}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.104)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\big(\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.105)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.106)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.107)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{n}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.108)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\big(\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.109)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{n}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.110)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.111)

Integration by parts using the identity
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\xi_{j}}{it2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
on the terms
(
6.107
)
and
(
6.111
)
gives

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.112)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.113)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.114)

and

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.115)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.116)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.117)

We now commence the estimation of all terms. Utilizing the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
, along with Lemma
2.6
, Lemma
2.4
, and the bounds in (
1.2
) and (
3.7
), we find

\displaystyle\eqref{km2-1.1}+\eqref{km2-1.1-2}+\|\eqref{km2-1.4}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.4-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2+k_{1}/4+k_{2}/4}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Then, the bilinear estimate
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
, Lemma
2.8
, (
2.7
) and (
3.2
) give

\displaystyle\eqref{km2-1.11}+\eqref{km2-1.11-2}+\eqref{km2-1.12}+\eqref{km2-1.12-2}+\|\eqref{km2-1.3}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.3-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big[\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\big(\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\big(\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big]\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k-M/2}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k-M/2}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}2^{k_{1}/2}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-1.5}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}g_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2+k_{2}-k+k_{1}/2}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Furthermore, employing the duality result stated in Lemma
2.4
, we can find a new set of bounds on the previous terms. We compute

\displaystyle\eqref{km2-1.1}+\eqref{km2-1.1-2}+\|\eqref{km2-1.4}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.4-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{1}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\eqref{km2-1.11}+\eqref{km2-1.11-2}+\eqref{km2-1.12}+\eqref{km2-1.12-2}+\|\eqref{km2-1.3}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.3-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+3\min\{k,k_{1}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2-2k+k_{1}}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-1.5}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-1.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+3\min\{k,k_{1}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2-2k+k_{1}}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

To summarize, we have

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

(6.118)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4+k_{1}-k}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

(6.119)

Using the interpolation result from Lemma
2.2
, we get

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-M/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4+(k_{1}-k)\alpha}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
.

If we look at a different set of bounds,

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{0+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

(6.120)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{1+iy}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2+k_{1}-k}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}},

(6.121)

we obtain for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k+M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{2}/2+(k_{1}-k)\alpha}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

Thus, combining the two bounds we got on
\|T^{1}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}+\|T^{2}_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+(k_{1}-k)\alpha}\min\{2^{-M/2-k_{2}/4+k_{1}/4}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}\}.

Finally, by substituting
f_{m}
for
g
and employing the bounds on
\|\hat{f}_{m,k}\|_{H^{\alpha}}
and
\|\hat{f}_{m,k}\|_{H^{1+\alpha}}
from equation (
3.3
), we arrive at the desired result.
∎

We move on to the estimation of the next term in the splitting of
J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

Lemma 6.10
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2.3}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-\alpha k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Define a family
T_{z}
of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
,

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}(\xi)=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

We want to show

T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

and

T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

are bounded for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
.

We estimate the operator
T_{0+iy}
by employing the bilinear estimate
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
, along with Lemma
2.4
and (
3.10
),

\displaystyle\|T_{0+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Next, for the operator
T_{1+iy}
, we compute

\displaystyle\|T_{1+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\partial_{\xi_{p}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{it\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.122)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\big(\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.123)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{p}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

(6.124)

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{p}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}.

(6.125)

Immediately, we see

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-2.7}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|T_{0+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k+\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k+\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}},

where the last line uses (
2.6
).

To handle the term (
6.122
), we proceed with integration by parts using
e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=-\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\xi_{j}}{it2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\big(-\sum_{j}\frac{\xi_{j}}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big)e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.126)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.127)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{j}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.128)

Now, we combine similar terms and perform estimations. Employing the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
, Lemma
2.6
, Lemma
2.4
, together with estimations (
1.2
), (
2.6
), (
3.2
) and (
3.13
), we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-2.2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-2.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\big(\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{j}}\big(\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/4+k_{1}/4}\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}2^{k_{1}}\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+k_{1}/4+3k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Applying the bilinear estimate
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
, together with Lemma
2.6
, Lemma
2.4
, bounds in (
1.2
), (
3.2
) and (
3.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-2.3}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{km2-2.5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2_{x}}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-k}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}(1+2^{k_{2}-k})\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+k_{1}-k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

Lastly, we use the bilinear estimate
L^{6}\times L^{3}\rightarrow L^{2}
and Lemma
2.4
to get

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-2.6}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{j}\partial_{\xi_{p}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{4c_{n}^{2}|\xi|^{4}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\qquad\times\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+2k_{2}-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|\nabla\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}.

In order to estimate the
L^{6}
norm, we employ Bernstein’s inequality in Lemma
2.11
, together with Lemma
2.8
and Lemma
2.13
to get

\displaystyle\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\gamma k/8}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k}\|_{L^{\frac{24}{\gamma+4}}_{x}}

(6.129)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\gamma k/8-M+\gamma M/8}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k}\|_{L^{\frac{24}{20-\gamma}}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-M+\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4}\|\nabla^{2}\hat{g}_{k}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

Hence, using the bound above with the estimations (
3.7
) and (
3.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2-2.6}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+2k_{2}-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-M+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{1}/4}\|e^{it\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\|\nabla\hat{f}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\|\nabla^{2}_{\xi}\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+k_{2}+\gamma k_{2}/4+\gamma k_{1}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}.

To conclude, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{1+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+k_{1}+\gamma k_{2,+}/4+\gamma k_{1,+}/4},

\displaystyle 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|T_{0+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8+\gamma k_{2}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}_{k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

By the interpolation result in Lemma
2.2
,

\displaystyle\|T_{\alpha}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{g}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}},

for any
\alpha\in(0,1)
.
Lastly, we plug
g=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\hat{f}_{m}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}
into the result above and use (
3.3
) to get

\displaystyle\bigg\|D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k)}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-\alpha k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2-k_{1}/2+3k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

∎

Now, we look at the final term in the splitting of
J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
.

Lemma 6.11
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If either
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}
or
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}=c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{km2.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-\alpha k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}+\alpha(k_{1}-k_{2})}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}+k_{2}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Since we restrict ourselves to the case when
c_{n}+c_{m}=0
for the term
J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we indeed have
\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)=2(c_{l}-c_{m})\xi_{l}+2c_{m}\eta_{l}
. As we perform a change of variable
\zeta=\xi-\eta
, we can rewrite
(
6.103
)
into

(
6.103
)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\zeta)}\frac{\xi_{m}((c_{l}-c_{m})\xi_{l}+c_{m}(\xi_{l}-\zeta_{l}))}{c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\zeta,\xi-\zeta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\zeta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi-\zeta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\zeta dt.

Based on this observation, we define a family
T_{z}
of operators on
\{z\in\mathbb{C}:0\leq\Re(z)\leq 1\}
as follows

\displaystyle T_{z}\hat{g}(\xi)=D^{z}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}((c_{l}-c_{m})\xi_{l}+c_{m}(\xi_{l}-\eta_{l}))}{2c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

It suffices to show that
T_{0+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

and

T_{1+iy}:L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{2}_{\xi}\rightarrow L^{2}_{\xi}

are bounded for all
y\in\mathbb{R}
with suitable bounds.

First, we look at the operator
T_{0+iy}
. The
L^{2}\times L^{\infty}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.6
, bounds in (
1.2
) and (
3.10
) yields

\displaystyle\|T_{0+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\xi-\eta)}\frac{\xi_{m}((c_{l}-c_{m})\xi_{l}+c_{m}(\xi_{l}-\eta_{l}))}{c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{g}_{k_{2}}(t,\xi-\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\xi_{m}((c_{l}-c_{m})\xi_{l}+c_{m}(\xi_{l}-\eta_{l}))}{c_{n}|\xi|^{2}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\xi-\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\min\{\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{c_{m}it\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}+M+k_{2}-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2}\}\|\nabla\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])L^{2}_{\xi}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+k_{2}+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}}\}\|\hat{g}_{k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1}_{\xi}}.

Next, we perform the following computation for

,

\displaystyle\|T_{1+it}\hat{g}\|_{L^{2}}

.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Combine the results we obtained in Lemma

, Lemma

and Lemma

. Given that

\displaystyle+2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-\alpha k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/4+\gamma k_{2,+}/4},

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k-\alpha k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-M/2-k_{2}/4-k_{1}/4},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2}\}

\displaystyle+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2},

if either

. Therefore, we proved

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
c_{l}=c_{m}\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+

(6.137)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\end{subarray}}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi_{k}^{1}\cup\chi_{k}^{2}\cup\chi_{k}^{3}}2^{\epsilon k_{-}-k/2-\gamma k+2k_{+}-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-M/2-k_{2}/4-k_{1}/4},

\displaystyle 2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2}\}+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/4-k_{1}/2+\gamma k_{1,+}/2},2^{M+3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

6.3.3
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}

In this section, we focus solely on the cases where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
. These conditions give us the lower bound
|\phi(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim 2^{k_{1}}
according to (
6.37
) and allows us to use the identity

e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=\frac{1}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}.

Recall the definition of
I^{m,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
and
J^{m,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
in (
6.4
) and (
6.7
). We perform integration by parts in the variable

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}_{\xi}I^{m,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{2}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{2},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{2},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.138)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{1}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{1},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{1},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.139)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.140)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.141)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.142)

and

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}_{\xi}J^{m,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{2}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{2},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{2},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.143)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{1}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{1}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{1},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{1},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.144)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.145)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.146)

\displaystyle-D^{\alpha}_{\xi}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.147)

We will divide the resulted terms into two groups, based on whether the derivative in
t
falls on the product
\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\xi)
, and estimate them respectively in Lemma
6.12
and
6.13
. For both groups, we will use Lemma
2.1
to bound the
\alpha
fractional derivative.

Lemma 6.12
.

Suppose
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
. If
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
and
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
, then

\displaystyle\|\eqref{f1-i}+\eqref{f2-i}+\eqref{f3-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{f1}+\eqref{f2}+\eqref{f3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle F_{1}(\xi)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

\displaystyle F^{q}_{1}(\xi)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

for
j=1,2
and

\displaystyle F_{2}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

\displaystyle F^{q}_{2}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

In order to apply Lemma
2.1
, we shall obtain bounds on
\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}
,
\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}
,
\|\nabla_{\xi}(F_{1}+F_{2})\|_{L^{2}}
, and
\|\nabla_{\xi}(F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2})\|_{L^{2}}
.

First, using the bilinear estimate
L^{\infty}\times L^{2}\rightarrow L^{2}
in Lemma
2.3
, along with Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, estimates in (
1.2
), (
3.10
), and (
3.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+k_{1}/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next, we compute the derivatives and get

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}F_{1}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.148)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.149)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.150)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{i\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)t^{2}_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

(6.151)

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}F^{q}_{1}=

(6.152)

(6.153)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.154)

(6.155)

(6.159)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}F^{q}_{2}=

(6.160)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\big)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.161)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.166)

(6.169)

(6.171)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.173)

(6.174)

, Lemma
