\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm2.11.2-i}+\eqref{jm2.11.2-2-i}+\eqref{jm2.12-i}+\eqref{jm2.12-2-i}+\eqref{jm2.3-i}+\eqref{jm2.3-2-i}\|_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\|\eqref{jm2.11.2}+\eqref{jm2.11.2-2}+\eqref{jm2.12}+\eqref{jm2.12-2}+\eqref{jm2.3}+\eqref{jm2.3-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}})+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k}(1+2^{k-k_{2}})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-k_{2}-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+k_{2}/2+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{M+3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k-k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+k_{2}+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Using the bilinear estimate
L^{6}\times L^{3}\rightarrow L^{2}
instead with the estimate in (
3.12
), we get

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm2.1-i}+\eqref{jm2.1-2-i}+\eqref{jm2.4-i}+\eqref{jm2.4-2-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm2.1}+\eqref{jm2.1-2}+\eqref{jm2.4}+\eqref{jm2.4-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k-k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+2k_{2}-k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Lastly, we may bound the remaining terms by the
L^{\infty}\times L^{2}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, along with Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
3.3
, Lemma
2.4
, estimations (
3.2
), (
3.7
) and (
3.10
),

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm2.5-i}+\eqref{jm2.5-2-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm2.5}+\eqref{jm2.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+\gamma k/4-k_{2}/2},2^{M+k_{2}+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we showed

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}(F_{1}+F_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{\xi}(F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k-k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+k_{2}+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

which imply

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}(F_{1}+F_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}_{\xi}(F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}(F_{1}+F_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla_{\xi}(F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2})\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{-k-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k+k_{2}/2+\gamma k/4},2^{M+k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

according to Lemma
2.1
.
∎

The estimation for the second group of terms is more intricate, as it requires bounding the
L^{p}
norms for the time derivatives
\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{k}(t,\xi)
and the mixed derivatives
\partial_{t}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}(t,\xi)
. We will state our results in two cases based on the relative size of
|\xi|\sim 2^{k}
compared to
t\sim 2^{M}
.

Lemma 6.13
.

Given
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}
,
c_{l}\neq c_{m}
, and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\eqref{f4-i}+\eqref{f5-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{f4}+\eqref{f5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\alpha k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-3k/2},2^{M-k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

if
M+2k\leq 0
, and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{f4-i}+\eqref{f5-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{f4}+\eqref{f5}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}/2-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\gamma k_{2,-}/2+\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

if
M+2k>0
.

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle F_{1}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

\displaystyle F^{q}_{1}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

\displaystyle F_{2}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

and

\displaystyle F^{q}_{2}(\xi)=\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

We shall use Lemma
2.1
to bound the
L^{2}
norm of
D^{\alpha}F_{i},D^{\alpha}F^{q}_{i}
for
i=1,2
.

First, we find a bound for
\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}
.

Using the bilinear estimate
L^{6}\times L^{3}\rightarrow L^{2}
, along with Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.13
, Lemma
3.3
, (
1.2
), and (
3.2
), we can estimate

\displaystyle\|F_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-2M-k_{1}/2-M/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-3k/2},2^{M-k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Similarly, by the
L^{\infty}\times L^{2}\rightarrow L^{2}
bilinear estimate, Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, together with (
1.2
), (
3.10
), and (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\|F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M+k_{1}/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-2k+\gamma k/4+k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Hence, we get

\displaystyle\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{(1+2^{\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4})2^{-M/2-3k/2},2^{M-k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

(6.176)

Next, we use integration by parts with the identity
\sum_{m}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{it|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}}\partial_{\eta_{m}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}
to obtain another bound for
\|F_{1}+F_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}
. We compute that

\displaystyle F_{1}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.177)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.178)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.179)

\displaystyle F^{q}_{1}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.180)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.181)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.182)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.183)

\displaystyle F_{2}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.184)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.185)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.186)

and

\displaystyle F^{q}_{2}(\xi)=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.187)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.188)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.189)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{m}}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

The bilinear estimate
L^{6}\times L^{3}\rightarrow L^{2}
, Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.13
, Lemma
3.3
, along with the estimates in (

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm20.1.1-i}+\eqref{jm20.2.1-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm20.1.1}+\eqref{jm20.2.1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-3k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-3k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-2M-k_{1}/2-M/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2-M+k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2-M+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{\epsilon k_{-}-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-7k/2},2^{-5k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm20.1.2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm20.2.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{m}}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k-k_{2}}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k-k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-2M-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2-M+k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2-M+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M-5k/2},2^{-3k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm20.1.3-i}+\eqref{jm20.2.4-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm20.1.3}+\eqref{jm20.2.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-M-\gamma k_{1}-M-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{3k_{2}/2-M-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M-k_{1}/2-M-\gamma k_{2}},(1+2^{M/2+k_{2}})2^{3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2-M-k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-3k-\gamma k_{-}-k_{2}/2},2^{-3k/2-\gamma k_{-}+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm20.1.4-i}+\eqref{jm20.2.3-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm20.1.4}+\eqref{jm20.2.3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{i|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\xi-\eta,\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-2k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}+

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\nabla_{\xi}\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)+

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\big(\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\big)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-k_{2}}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\partial_{t}{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-k_{2}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\big(\min\{2^{-2M-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2+k_{2}/2},2^{-M+3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-5M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+k_{2}/2+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{-M+3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}}\big(\min\{2^{-k_{1}/2},2^{M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}}\}

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}-k_{2}/2+\gamma k_{1}/4},2^{M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm2-2.1-i}+\eqref{jm2-2.4-i}+\eqref{jm2-2.1-2-i}+\eqref{jm2-2.4-2-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm2-2.1}+\eqref{jm2-2.4}+\eqref{jm2-2.1-2}+\eqref{jm2-2.4-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}q(\eta,\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\gamma k_{1}}\big(\min\{2^{-M-\gamma k_{1}-M-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{3k_{2}/2-M-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{1}+k_{2}/2}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad+\min\{2^{-M/2+\gamma M/8-k_{1}+\gamma k_{1}/4-2M-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{1}/2-M+k_{2}/2}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{1}+\gamma k_{2}}\big(\min\{2^{-\gamma k_{1}-k_{2}/2},(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{M-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{1}+2k_{2}}\}+

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k-\gamma k_{-}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-3k/2+2k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2},

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{jm2-2.5-i}+\eqref{jm2-2.5-2-i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{jm2-2.5}+\eqref{jm2-2.5-2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle 2^{2M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\eta_{n}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|^{2}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{3}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}+

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\big(\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{6}_{x}}2^{k_{2}/2}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}},2^{3k_{2}/2}\|\partial_{t}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\}+

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{2M-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\gamma k_{1}}\big(\min\{2^{-2M-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2-k_{2}/2},2^{3k_{2}/2-k_{2}/2-M+k_{1}/2}\}+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\min\{2^{-M-k_{1}/2-M-\gamma k_{2}},(1+2^{M/2+k_{2}})2^{-M+3k_{2}/2+k_{1}/2-k_{2}/2-\gamma k_{2}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-k-2k_{1,+}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}+\gamma k_{1}}\big(\min\{2^{-k_{1}/2},2^{M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}}\}+\min\{2^{-k_{1}/2-\gamma k_{2}},(1+2^{M/2+k_{2}})2^{M+k_{1}/2+k_{2}-\gamma k_{2}}\}\big)\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\gamma k_{2,-}}\min\{2^{-M/2-5k/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we showed

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{\xi}(F_{1}+F_{2})\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{\xi}(F^{q}_{1}+F^{q}_{2})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M/2+k})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}-\gamma k_{2,-}}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k-k_{2}/2},2^{M-k/2+k_{2}}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Using Lemma
2.1
and (
6.176
), when

.

6.3.4
D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
where
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}

Unlike the situation where we only consider
c_{m}+c_{n}=0
for the term
J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, which allows use to handle the cases when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
together with the cases when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}
in Section
6.3.2
. For
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we failed to obtain a lower bound for
|\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)|
when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
. Thus, we treat this case separately here in this section.

Similarly to
[
4
, Theorem 2]
, we define
P(\xi,\eta)=\frac{1}{2}(c_{l}c_{m}+c_{l}c_{n}-c_{m}c_{n}+1)(\frac{c_{m}}{c_{m}+c_{n}}\xi-\eta)
, then we have the identity

(\partial_{t}+\frac{P(\xi,\eta)}{t}\cdot\nabla_{\eta})e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}=iZ(\xi,\eta)e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)},

(6.223)

where

\displaystyle Z(\xi,\eta)=

\displaystyle\phi(\xi,\eta)+P(\xi,\eta)\cdot\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c_{l}c_{m}+c_{l}c_{n}-c_{m}c_{n}}{c_{m}+c_{n}}\big((1+c^{2}_{m})|\xi|^{2}-2c_{m}(c_{m}+c_{n})\xi\cdot\eta+(c_{m}+c_{n})^{2}|\eta|^{2}\big).

This polynomial
P
is chosen to ensure the
Z
bounded from below when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
.
Since

2|c_{m}(c_{m}+c_{n})\xi\cdot\eta|\leq c_{m}^{2}|\xi|^{2}+(c_{m}+c_{n})^{2}|\eta|^{2},

we indeed have
|Z(\xi,\eta)|\gtrsim|\xi|^{2}\sim 2^{2k}
. However, we notice that this is only true given
c_{l}c_{m}+c_{l}c_{n}-c_{m}c_{n}\neq 0
. Hence, we will assume this for now and address the case when
\frac{1}{c_{l}}=\frac{1}{c_{m}}+\frac{1}{c_{n}}
at the end of this section.

Now, we use the identity (
6.223
) to integrate
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
by parts and get

\displaystyle I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\partial_{t}+\frac{P(\xi,\eta)}{t}\cdot\nabla_{\eta})e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1,2}(-1)^{j}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.224)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.225)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.226)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.227)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.228)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.229)

\displaystyle-\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.230)

We observe that the power of

in

is lowered by

while

is expanded into a sum of terms. In the lemma below, we will first address the terms where the derivatives of

and

did not fall on the product

.

Lemma 6.14
.

Given
t_{1},t_{2}\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]
,
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
,
\frac{1}{c_{l}}\neq\frac{1}{c_{m}}+\frac{1}{c_{n}}
, and
\sup_{t\in[1,T]}\|f\|_{Z}\leq\epsilon_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.1}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.2}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-3k/2+\alpha M+\alpha k},2^{M+3k/2-\alpha k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Proof.

In order to use Lemma
2.1
, we need bounds on
\|\eqref{chi3.1}+\eqref{chi3.2}\|_{L^{2}}
and
\|\nabla\eqref{chi3.1}+\nabla\eqref{chi3.2}\|_{L^{2}}
when
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}=\{|k_{1}-k_{2}|\leq a,|k-k_{1}|\leq a+2\}
.

First, using the bilinear estimate
L^{4}\times L^{4}\rightarrow L^{2}
in Lemma
2.3
, Lemma
2.5
Lemma
2.4
, together with the estimations in (
3.13
), and (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\|\eqref{chi3.1}+\eqref{chi3.2}+\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigg\|\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt\bigg\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{4}_{x}},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-k_{1}/4-k_{2}/4},2^{3\min\{k,k_{2}\}/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-3k/2},2^{M+3k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Next, we compute

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{chi3.1}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.231)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.232)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\frac{t_{j}\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta

(6.233)

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it_{j}\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{it_{j}^{2}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t_{j},\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t_{j},\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta,

(6.234)

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{chi3.2}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.235)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.236)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.237)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\frac{it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt,

(6.238)

and

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\eqref{chi3.4}=

\displaystyle\partial_{\xi_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.239)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{m}}\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.240)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.241)

\displaystyle+\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.242)

Applying the bilinear estimate
L^{18/7}\times L^{9}\rightarrow L^{2}
, along with Lemma
2.5
, Lemma
2.8
, Lemma
2.4
, Lemma
2.13
, (
3.2
), and (
3.7
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2-2.1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.1-2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.1-3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M}\big(\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/7}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9}_{x}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad 2^{3k/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k}\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/2}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/11}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9/8}_{x}},2^{3k/2}\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{M-k_{2}-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2-5k_{1}/6-2k_{2}/3},2^{3k/2-k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-5k/2},2^{M+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\eqref{im2-2.12}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.12*}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.12-2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.12-2*}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.12-3}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.12-3*}\|_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\|\eqref{im2-2.2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.2-2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\eqref{im2-2.2-3}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\big(2^{M}\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+2^{M}\|\nabla\psi_{k}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+2^{M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+2^{2M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\frac{\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}+

\displaystyle+2^{2M}\|\mathcal{F}^{-1}\partial_{\xi_{m}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\eta_{n}}\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}\tilde{\psi}_{k_{1}}(\xi-\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k_{2}}(\eta)\tilde{\psi}_{k}(\xi)\|_{L^{1}}\big)\times

\displaystyle\times\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/7}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9}_{x}},2^{3k/2}\|e^{ic_{m}t\Delta}f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|e^{ic_{n}t\Delta}f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{M-2k}+2^{2M})\sup_{t\in[2^{M-1},2^{M}]}\min\{2^{-3M/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{18/11}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{9/8}_{x}},2^{3k/2}\|f_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|f_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M+2k})2^{M-2k-2k_{1,+}+\gamma k_{1}-2k_{2,+}+\gamma k_{2}}\min\{2^{-3M/2+k_{1}/6-2k_{2}/3},2^{3k/2+k_{1}/2+k_{2}/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{M+2k})2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-5k/2},2^{M+k/2}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Thus, we have when
M\leq-2k
,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{chi3.1}+\eqref{chi3.2}+\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+3k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla\eqref{chi3.1}+\nabla\eqref{chi3.2}+\nabla\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Lemma
2.1
implies

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.1}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.2}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M+3k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Furthermore when
M>-2k
,

\displaystyle\|\eqref{chi3.1}+\eqref{chi3.2}+\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M/2-3k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla\eqref{chi3.1}+\nabla\eqref{chi3.2}+\nabla\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k+M/2-k/2}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

By Lemma
2.1
, we get

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.1}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.2}+D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.4}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-M/2-3k/2+\alpha M+\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

∎

As a result of Lemma
6.14
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}\big\|D^{\alpha}\big[\eqref{chi3.1}+\eqref{chi3.2}+\eqref{chi3.4}\big]\big\|_{L^{2}}

(6.243)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+\alpha M+2\alpha k},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{M\leq-2k}2^{\gamma k+M+2k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{-2k<M\leq\log T}2^{\alpha(M+2k)-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M/2-k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

For the remaining terms in the expansion of
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
, we will estimate directly by applying Lemma
6.5
.

Let
\hat{g}=\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l}
and
m_{d}=\frac{\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.3.1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{M/2-2k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{2M+k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.3.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{M/2-2k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{2M+k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}.

From Lemma
3.3
and Lemma
3.4
, we know
\|\partial_{t}\hat{f}_{l,k}\|_{H^{\alpha}}\lesssim 2^{-M-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k+k/2-\alpha k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.3.1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.3.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k+k/2-\alpha k}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-2k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
.

Then, take
\hat{g}=\partial_{\xi_{n}}\hat{f}_{l}
and
m_{d}=\frac{P_{n}(\xi,\eta)\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)}{Z(\xi,\eta)}
. Employing Lemma
6.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.5.1}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}

and

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.5.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\|\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{1+\alpha}_{\xi}}.

Using the bound in (
3.3
) for the Sobolev norms, we have

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.5.1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{chi3.5.2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3},

for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
.
Hence, we may conclude

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}\big\|D^{\alpha}\big[\eqref{chi3.3.1}+\eqref{chi3.3.2}+\eqref{chi3.5.1}+\eqref{chi3.5.2}\big]\big\|_{L^{2}}

(6.244)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{M\leq-2k}(1+2^{\alpha\gamma k})2^{(1-\alpha)\gamma k+M+2k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{M>-2k}(1+2^{\alpha\gamma k})2^{\alpha(M+2k)-2k_{+}+(1-\alpha)\gamma k-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2},

given
\alpha+(1-\alpha)\gamma/8<1/2
, i.e.
\alpha<1/2-\gamma/(16-2\gamma)
.

Finally, we deal with the case when
\frac{1}{c_{l}}=\frac{1}{c_{m}}+\frac{1}{c_{n}}
. Since we have

\nabla_{\xi}\phi(\xi,\eta)=\frac{c_{l}-c_{m}}{c_{m}}\nabla_{\eta}\phi(\xi,\eta),

where
\frac{c_{l}-c_{m}}{c_{m}}\neq 0
, we can rewrite
I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}
as

\displaystyle I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}=

\displaystyle\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\xi_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{c_{l}-c_{m}}{c_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}it\partial_{\eta_{l}}\phi(\xi,\eta)\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{l}-c_{m}}{c_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt

(6.245)

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{l}-c_{m}}{c_{m}}\int_{t_{1}}^{t_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}e^{it\phi(\xi,\eta)}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}(t,\xi-\eta)\partial_{\eta_{l}}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}(t,\eta)\psi_{k}(\xi)d\eta dt.

(6.246)

Furthermore, as a result of Lemma
6.5
and (
3.3
), we know for
(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}
,

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}\leq

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{im2chi3extra1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|D^{\alpha}\eqref{im2chi3extra2}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}\times

\displaystyle\times(\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{m,k_{1}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}}+\|\nabla_{\xi}\hat{f}_{n,k_{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}([2^{M-1},2^{M}])H^{\alpha}_{\xi}})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-4k_{+}+2\gamma k-k/2-\alpha k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}\big\|D^{\alpha}\big[\eqref{im2chi3extra1}+\eqref{im2chi3extra2}\big]\big\|_{L^{2}}

(6.247)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}(1+2^{\alpha(M+2k)})2^{-2k_{+}+\gamma k}\min\{2^{-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)},2^{M+2k}\}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{M\leq-2k}2^{\gamma k+M+2k}\epsilon_{1}^{2}+\sum_{M>-2k}2^{\alpha(M+2k)-2k_{+}+\gamma k-M/2-k+(1-\alpha)(\gamma M/8+\gamma k/4)}\epsilon_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2},

given that
\alpha+(1-\alpha)\gamma/8<1/2
.

Therefore, combining the results in (
6.243
) and (
6.244
) with (
6.99
), (
6.137
), (
6.222
), and (
6.247
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq M\leq\log T}\sup_{2^{M-1}\leq t_{1}\leq t_{2}\leq 2^{M}}2^{2k_{+}-\gamma k+k/2+\alpha k}\bigg(\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
c_{m}\neq c_{l}\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0\\
c_{m}=c_{l}\end{subarray}}\big)A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}\neq 0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}I^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\big(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
c_{m}\neq c_{l}\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}c_{m}+c_{n}=0\\
c_{m}=c_{l}\end{subarray}}\big)A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{1}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{c_{m}+c_{n}=0}A_{lmn}\sum_{(k_{1},k_{2})\in\chi^{2}_{k}\cup\chi^{3}_{k}}\|D^{\alpha}J^{M,2}_{k,k_{1},k_{2}}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon_{1}^{2}

as desired.
